([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/ribo-seq-orf-detection) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **ribo-seq-riboseq-preprocessing**: Preprocess ribosome profiling data including adapter trimming, size selection, rRNA removal, and alignment. Use when preparing Ribo-seq reads for downstream analysis of translation. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/ribo-seq-riboseq-preprocessing) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **ribo-seq-ribosome-periodicity**: Validate Ribo-seq data quality by checking 3-nucleotide periodicity and calculating P-site offsets. Use when assessing library quality or determining read offsets for downstream analysis. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/ribo-seq-ribosome-periodicity) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **ribo-seq-ribosome-stalling**: Detect ribosome pausing and stalling sites from Ribo-seq data at codon resolution. Use when studying translational regulation, identifying pause sites, or analyzing codon-specific translation dynamics. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/ribo-seq-ribosome-stalling) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **ribo-seq-translation-efficiency**: Calculate translation efficiency (TE) as the ratio of ribosome occupancy to mRNA abundance. Use when comparing translational regulation between conditions or identifying genes with altered translation independent of transcription. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/ribo-seq-translation-efficiency) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **riboclaw-visualization**: Produce RiboClaw-friendly structured process visualization output (chain + step cards + image artifacts). Use when the user asks to visualize reasoning, show processing DAG/chain, render step-by-step execution, or drive the RiboClaw frontend timeline. ([shuotong-song/cytoclaw-skills](https://github.com/shuotong-song/cytoclaw-skills/tree/HEAD/workspace/skills/riboclaw-visualization) · 2 copies)
- **ribodepletion-efficiency-comparison**: Use when a pair of metatranscriptome samples (ribodepleted vs. non-ribodepleted, e.g. MG-RAST samples 4015 vs. 4012) must be compared to assess whether rRNA depletion is taxonomically unbiased, or when residual rRNA reads might distort organism abundance estimates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/ribodepletion-efficiency-comparison))
- **ribonuclease-h-domain-recognition**: Use when candidate IS nucleotide sequences (e.g. ITR-flanked contigs from Palidis) must be confirmed as encoding transposase-family proteins before inclusion in an insertion sequence catalogue. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/ribonuclease-h-domain-recognition))
- **riboseq-pipeline**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Workflow_Management/workflows/riboseq-pipeline) · ★7)
- **riboseq-pipeline-configuration**: Use when starting a Ribo-seq analysis project from raw FastQ files with RiboDoc: building the project folder, naming *.fastq.gz samples, choosing genome/rRNA fasta references, and setting config.yaml options (RPF lengths 25-35 nt, p-val, logFC, riboWaltz vs TRiP). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/riboseq-pipeline-configuration))
